{"parentUuid":"c4bfb7ce-c483-404d-b221-692ba448ca4a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c4d76306-3d6f-4795-97b1-6d7124e0032e","agentId":"afca6772ad1d0c8df","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WHPcZotBoprinhQCrJevaZ","type":"tool_result","content":"630\t\n631\t/**\n632\t * Spendable credit balance for a single license.\n633\t *\n634\t * The credit_grants rows are the source of truth; credit_balances is only a\n635\t * cache of their summed remaining_amount. To avoid ever reporting credits the\n636\t * user cannot actually consume (or hiding credits they can), this returns the\n637\t * authoritative grant total and lazily heals the cache when the two have\n638\t * drifted. The cheap read path (cache hit that already matches) does no write.\n639\t */\n640\texport async function getCreditBalance(userId: string): Promise<number> {\n641\t  const [cachedRow, grantsTotal] = await Promise.all([\n642\t    db.query.creditBalances.findFirst({\n643\t      where: eq(creditBalances.userId, userId),\n644\t    }),\n645\t    getActiveGrantsTotal(db, userId),\n646\t  ]);\n647\t\n648\t  const cached = cachedRow ? Number(cachedRow.balance) : null;\n649\t  if (cached !== grantsTotal) {\n650\t    // Drift detected (e.g. a legacy balance row with no matching grants, or a\n651\t    // partially-applied write): heal the cache to the authoritative total.\n652\t    await db.transaction((tx) => reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId));\n653\t  }\n654\t\n655\t  return grantsTotal;\n656\t}\n657\t\n658\t/**\n659\t * Spendable credit balances for many accounts at once (dashboard / admin list).\n660\t *\n661\t * Like {@link getCreditBalance}, the credit_grants rows are the source of truth\n662\t * and credit_balances is only a cache. This reads the authoritative grant total\n663\t * (SUM of remaining_amount across each account's still-active grants) directly,\n664\t * so an account whose cached balance has drifted above its true grant total never\n665\t * shows phantom credits on the dashboard. Accounts with no active grants default\n666\t * to 0. (The cache still self-heals on the next single-account ledger\n667\t * operation; this read path simply never relies on it.)\n668\t */\n669\texport async function getCreditBalancesForUsers(userIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, number>> {\n670\t  const map = new Map<string, number>();\n671\t  if (userIds.length === 0) return map;\n672\t  // Default every requested id to 0 so accounts with no active grants are\n673\t  // explicitly present in the map.\n674\t  for (const id of userIds) {\n675\t    map.set(id, 0);\n676\t  }\n677\t  const rows = await db\n678\t    .select({\n679\t      userId: creditGrants.userId,\n680\t      total: sql<string>`COALESCE(SUM(${creditGrants.remainingAmount}), 0)`,\n681\t    })\n682\t    .from(creditGrants)\n683\t    .where(\n684\t      and(\n685\t        inArray(creditGrants.userId, userIds),\n686\t        eq(creditGrants.status, \"active\"),\n687\t        sql`${creditGrants.remainingAmount} > 0`,\n688\t        sql`(${creditGrants.expiresAt} IS NULL OR ${creditGrants.expiresAt} > now())`\n689\t      )\n690\t    )\n691\t    .groupBy(creditGrants.userId);\n692\t  for (const row of rows) {\n693\t    map.set(row.userId, Number(row.total ?? 0));\n694\t  }\n695\t  return map;\n696\t}\n697\t\n698\t/**\n699\t * Attach each license's pooled account balance. Credits are pooled per account,\n700\t * so balances are resolved by the licenses' distinct owning users and every\n701\t * license of an account reports that one shared balance. Returns [] for empty\n702\t * input without issuing a query.\n703\t */\n704\tasync function attachPooledCredits<T extends { userId: string }>(\n705\t  licenses: T[]\n706\t): Promise<Array<T & { credits: number }>> {\n707\t  if (licenses.length === 0) return [];\n708\t  const balanceMap = await getCreditBalancesForUsers(\n709\t    Array.from(new Set(licenses.map((l) => l.userId)))\n710\t  );\n711\t  return licenses.map((license) => ({\n712\t    ...license,\n713\t    credits: balanceMap.get(license.userId) || 0,\n714\t  }));\n715\t}\n716\t\n717\texport interface CreditGrantHistoryRow {\n718\t  id: string;\n719\t  userId: string;\n720\t  createdAt: Date;\n721\t  expiresAt: Date | null;\n722\t  originalAmount: number;\n723\t  remainingAmount: number;\n724\t  status: string;\n725\t}\n726\t\n727\t/**\n728\t * Paid credit-pack grants for the given licenses, newest-first.\n729\t *"}]},"uuid":"ff7d70b1-9e0e-4dbc-81df-dc46e1b26dd5","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:44:17.169Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts","content":"\n/**\n * Spendable credit balance for a single license.\n *\n * The credit_grants rows are the source of truth; credit_balances is only a\n * cache of their summed remaining_amount. To avoid ever reporting credits the\n * user cannot actually consume (or hiding credits they can), this returns the\n * authoritative grant total and lazily heals the cache when the two have\n * drifted. The cheap read path (cache hit that already matches) does no write.\n */\nexport async function getCreditBalance(userId: string): Promise<number> {\n  const [cachedRow, grantsTotal] = await Promise.all([\n    db.query.creditBalances.findFirst({\n      where: eq(creditBalances.userId, userId),\n    }),\n    getActiveGrantsTotal(db, userId),\n  ]);\n\n  const cached = cachedRow ? Number(cachedRow.balance) : null;\n  if (cached !== grantsTotal) {\n    // Drift detected (e.g. a legacy balance row with no matching grants, or a\n    // partially-applied write): heal the cache to the authoritative total.\n    await db.transaction((tx) => reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId));\n  }\n\n  return grantsTotal;\n}\n\n/**\n * Spendable credit balances for many accounts at once (dashboard / admin list).\n *\n * Like {@link getCreditBalance}, the credit_grants rows are the source of truth\n * and credit_balances is only a cache. This reads the authoritative grant total\n * (SUM of remaining_amount across each account's still-active grants) directly,\n * so an account whose cached balance has drifted above its true grant total never\n * shows phantom credits on the dashboard. Accounts with no active grants default\n * to 0. (The cache still self-heals on the next single-account ledger\n * operation; this read path simply never relies on it.)\n */\nexport async function getCreditBalancesForUsers(userIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, number>> {\n  const map = new Map<string, number>();\n  if (userIds.length === 0) return map;\n  // Default every requested id to 0 so accounts with no active grants are\n  // explicitly present in the map.\n  for (const id of userIds) {\n    map.set(id, 0);\n  }\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      userId: creditGrants.userId,\n      total: sql<string>`COALESCE(SUM(${creditGrants.remainingAmount}), 0)`,\n    })\n    .from(creditGrants)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        inArray(creditGrants.userId, userIds),\n        eq(creditGrants.status, \"active\"),\n        sql`${creditGrants.remainingAmount} > 0`,\n        sql`(${creditGrants.expiresAt} IS NULL OR ${creditGrants.expiresAt} > now())`\n      )\n    )\n    .groupBy(creditGrants.userId);\n  for (const row of rows) {\n    map.set(row.userId, Number(row.total ?? 0));\n  }\n  return map;\n}\n\n/**\n * Attach each license's pooled account balance. Credits are pooled per account,\n * so balances are resolved by the licenses' distinct owning users and every\n * license of an account reports that one shared balance. Returns [] for empty\n * input without issuing a query.\n */\nasync function attachPooledCredits<T extends { userId: string }>(\n  licenses: T[]\n): Promise<Array<T & { credits: number }>> {\n  if (licenses.length === 0) return [];\n  const balanceMap = await getCreditBalancesForUsers(\n    Array.from(new Set(licenses.map((l) => l.userId)))\n  );\n  return licenses.map((license) => ({\n    ...license,\n    credits: balanceMap.get(license.userId) || 0,\n  }));\n}\n\nexport interface CreditGrantHistoryRow {\n  id: string;\n  userId: string;\n  createdAt: Date;\n  expiresAt: Date | null;\n  originalAmount: number;\n  remainingAmount: number;\n  status: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Paid credit-pack grants for the given licenses, newest-first.\n *","numLines":100,"startLine":630,"totalLines":1024}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c4bfb7ce-c483-404d-b221-692ba448ca4a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

The magic-link plugin flips emailVerified to true on the first\n  // successful sign-in, which is the only point where ownership is verified.\n  const userId = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const [newUser] = await db\n    .insert(user)\n    .values({\n      id: userId,\n      name: metadata?.name ?? email.split(\"@\")[0],\n      email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),\n      emailVerified: false,\n    })\n    .returning();\n  if (!newUser) return null;\n\n  // Also insert an account record so the user can sign in via magic link\n  await db.insert(account).values({\n    id: `magic-link-${userId}`,\n    accountId: userId,\n    providerId: \"magic-link\",\n    userId: userId,\n    createdAt: new Date(),\n    updatedAt: new Date(),\n  });\n\n  return { id: newUser.id, email: newUser.email };\n}\n\nexport async function getUserById(id: string): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n    where: eq(user.id, id),\n  });\n  return existing ? { id: existing.id, email: existing.email } : null;\n}\n\nexport async function getUserByEmail(email: string): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n    where: eq(user.email, email.toLowerCase().trim()),\n  });\n  return existing ? { id: existing.id, email: existing.email } : null;\n}\n\nexport async function getUsersByIds(ids: string[]): Promise<Map<string, UserResult>> {\n  if (ids.length === 0) return new Map();\n  const rows = await db.query.user.findMany({\n    where: inArray(user.id, ids),\n  });\n  const map = new Map<string, UserResult>();\n  for (const row of rows) {\n    map.set(row.id, { id: row.id, email: row.email });\n  }\n  return map;\n}\n\n/**\n * Move a customer to a new email. The caller should pre-check uniqueness; the\n * user.email UNIQUE constraint is the final guard (a colliding write throws\n * Postgres error code 23505, which the caller maps to a conflict).\n */\nexport async function updateCustomerEmail(\n  userId: string,\n  newEmail: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  const email = newEmail.toLowerCase().trim();\n  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    // Reset emailVerified: the new address has not been proven to belong to\n    // the account holder (matches the false default for admin/import-created\n    // users). The magic-link plugin re-verifies on the next sign-in.\n    await tx\n      .update(user)\n      .set({ email, emailVerified: false, updatedAt: new Date() })\n      .where(eq(user.id, userId));\n    await tx\n      .update(accountKeys)\n      .set({ email })\n      .where(eq(accountKeys.userId, userId));\n  });\n}\n","numLines":100,"startLine":925,"totalLines":1024}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"deb9f5d6-f2e1-4e71-b145-7d6f5ec7f1c0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

\"./auth\";\n\nexport const creditGrants = pgTable(\n  \"credit_grants\",\n  {\n    id: uuid(\"id\").defaultRandom().primaryKey(),\n    userId: text(\"user_id\")\n      .notNull()\n      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),\n    sourceType: text(\"source_type\").notNull(),\n    sourceId: text(\"source_id\").notNull(),\n    originalAmount: numeric(\"original_amount\", { precision: 20, scale: 2 })\n      .notNull(),\n    remainingAmount: numeric(\"remaining_amount\", { precision: 20, scale: 2 })\n      .notNull(),\n    refundedAmount: numeric(\"refunded_amount\", { spendable\n    // balance. Null means never expires (e.g. trial credits). Enforcement is a\n    // lazy filter (`expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now()`) in the read/spend\n    // queries — there is no cron, so an expired row stays status='active' but is\n    // excluded from balance and spend. New paid/minted grants are stamped\n    // created_at + 365 days; see grantCreditLotInTransaction in db-layer.ts.\n    expiresAt: timestamp(\"expires_at\", { withTimezone: true }),\n    createdAt: timestamp(\"created_at\", { withTimezone: true })\n      .notNull()\n      .defaultNow(),\n    updatedAt: timestamp(\"updated_at\", { withTimezone: true })\n      .notNull()\n      .defaultNow(),\n  },\n  (table) => [\n    uniqueIndex(\"credit_grants_source_unique\").on(\n      table.sourceType,\n      table.sourceId,\n    ),\n    index(\"credit_grants_user_source_remaining_idx\").on(\n      table.userId,\n      table.sourceType,\n      table.remainingAmount,\n    ),\n    // Supports the per-account active-and-unexpired scan used by balance/spend.\n    index(\"credit_grants_user_status_expires_idx\").on(\n      table.userId,\n      table.status,\n      table.expiresAt,\n    ),\n  ],\n);\n\nexport const creditGrantsRelations = relations(creditGrants, ({ one }) => ({\n  user: one(user, {\n    fields: [creditGrants.userId],\n    references: [user.id],\n  }),\n}));\n","numLines":69,"startLine":1,"totalLines":69}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2015abcf-7732-4b79-9266-973125a4b2c1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{ createAuthEndpoint } from \"better-auth/api\";\nimport { setSessionCookie } from \"better-auth/cookies\";\nimport { eq } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\n\nimport { findAccountByKey } from \"./db-layer\";\nimport { sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect } from \"./license-key-redirect\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/src/db\";\nimport { user } from \"@/src/db/schema/auth\";\n\nexport const licenseKeyPlugin = () => ({\n  id: \"license-key\",\n  endpoints: {\n    signInLicenseKey: createAuthEndpoint(\n      \"/sign-in/license-key\",\n      {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        requireHeaders: true,\n        body: z.object({\n          licenseKey: z.string(),\n          callbackURL: z.string().optional(),\n        }),\n      },\n      async (ctx) => {\n        const { licenseKey, callbackURL } = ctx.body;\n\n        const license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey);\n\n        if (!license || license.status !== \"granted\") {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Invalid or inactive license key.\" },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        if (!license.userId) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const [foundUser] = await db\n          .select()\n          .from(user)\n          .where(eq(user.id, license.userId))\n          .limit(1);\n\n        if (!foundUser) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const session = await ctx.context.internalAdapter.createSession(\n          foundUser.id,\n        );\n\n        if (!session) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Failed to create session.\" },\n            { status: 500 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        await setSessionCookie(ctx, { session, user: foundUser });\n\n        const ipAddress =\n          ctx.headers?.get(\"x-forwarded-for\")?.split(\",\")[0]?.trim() ??\n          \"unknown\";\n        const userAgent = ctx.headers?.get(\"user-agent\") ?? \"unknown\";\n        console.log(\n          `[license-key-sign-in] user=${foundUser.id} license=${license.id} ip=${ipAddress} ua=\"${userAgent}\"`,\n        );\n\n        return ctx.json({ redirect: sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect(callbackURL) });\n      },\n    ),\n  },\n  rateLimit: [\n    {\n      pathMatcher(path: string) {\n        return path === \"/sign-in/license-key\";\n      },\n      window: 60,\n      max: 5,\n    },\n  ],\n});\n","numLines":98,"startLine":1,"totalLines":98}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f2a8ea0d-739d-4c9a-b47f-b8156a926ed6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

[response.error] : []);\n=== middleware ===\nimport createMiddleware from \"next-intl/middleware\";\r\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\r\n\r\nimport { routing } from \"./src/i18n/routing\";\r\nimport { defaultLocale, locales } from \"./src/i18n/locales\";\r\nimport { sanitizeReturnTo } from \"./src/lib/license-key-redirect\";\r\n\r\nconst intlMiddleware = createMiddleware(routing);\r\nconst localePattern = locales\r\n  .map((locale) => locale.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, \"\\\\$&\"))\r\n  .join(\"|\");\r\nconst LOCALE_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})(\\\\/|$)`);\r\nconst USER_ROUTE_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user`);\r\nconst USER_SIGN_IN_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/sign-in`);\r\nconst USER_AUTH_SIGN_OUT_REGEX = new RegExp(\r\n  `^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/auth\\\\/sign-out`,\r\n);\r\nconst USER_CUSTOMERS_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/customers`);\r\n\r\nconst getPathLocale = (pathname: string) => {\r\n  const match = pathname.match(LOCALE_REGEX);\r\n  return match?.[1] ?? defaultLocale;\r\n};\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Check if Better Auth session cookie is present.\r\n * For middleware, we only check cookie presence (no HTTP round-trip).\r\n * Actual session validation happens in the API layer.\r\n *\r\n * Better Auth prefixes cookies with \"__Secure-\" in production when\r\n * baseURL starts with \"https://\", so we check both names.\r\n */\r\nfunction hasSessionCookie(request: NextRequest): boolean {\r\n  return !!(\r\n    request.cookies.get(\"better-auth.session_token\")?.value ||\r\n    request.cookies.get(\"__Secure-better-auth.session_token\")?.value\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Get full Better Auth session via API call.\r\n * Only used for admin routes that need the user's email.\r\n */\r\nasync function getBetterAuthSession(request: NextRequest) {\r\n  const sessionCookie =\r\n    request.cookies.get(\"__Secure-better-auth.session_token\") ||\r\n    request.cookies.get(\"better-auth.session_token\");\r\n  if (!sessionCookie?.value) return null;\r\n\r\n  try {\r\n    const baseUrl = request.nextUrl.origin;\r\n    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/get-session`, {\r\n      headers: {\r\n        cookie: request.headers.get(\"cookie\") || \"\",\r\n      },\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    if (!response.ok) return null;\r\n\r\n    const data = await response.json();\r\n    return data?.user ?? null;\r\n  } catch {\r\n    return null;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Middleware that handles:\r\n * 1. next-intl locale routing (adds locale prefix)\r\n * 2. Better Auth session checking\r\n * 3. User route protection (unified portal for customers and admins)\r\n */\r\nexport default async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {\r\n  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;\r\n\r\n  // Check if this is a user route (matches /<locale>/user/*)\r\n  const isUserRoute = USER_ROUTE_REGEX.test(pathname);\r\n  const isUserSignIn = USER_SIGN_IN_REGEX.test(pathname);\r\n  const isUserAuthSignOut = USER_AUTH_SIGN_OUT_REGEX.test(pathname);\r\n  const isUserCustomers = USER_CUSTOMERS_REGEX.test(pathname);\r\n\r\n  // =============================================================\r\n  // USER ROUTES - Unified portal for customers and admins\r\n  // =============================================================\r\n\r\n  // For /user/customers, require admin access\r\n  if (isUserCustomers) {\r\n    const user = await getBetterAuthSession(request);\r\n\r\n    // If not authenticated, redirect to user sign-in\r\n    if (!user) {\r\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\r\n      const signInUrl = new URL(`/${locale}/user/sign-in`, request.url);\r\n      signInUrl.searchParams.set(\"returnTo\", pathname);\r\n      return NextResponse.redirect(signInUrl);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // Must be admin to access customers page\r\n    if (user.role !== \"admin\") {\r\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\r\n      return NextResponse.redirect(\r\n        new URL(`/${locale}/user/dashboard`, request.url)\r\n      );\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // User is authenticated and is admin - run intl middleware\r\n    const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\r\n    response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\r\n    return response;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // For other user routes (except sign-in, sign-out), check authentication via cookie presence\r\n  if (\r\n    isUserRoute &&\r\n    !isUserSignIn &&\r\n    !isUserAuthSignOut\r\n  ) {\r\n    if (!hasSessionCookie(request)) {\r\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\r\n      const signInUrl = new URL(`/${locale}/user/sign-in`, request.url);\r\n      signInUrl.searchParams.set(\"returnTo\", pathname);\r\n      return NextResponse.redirect(signInUrl);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // User has session cookie - {"parentUuid":"d119fe36-23ef-4618-872f-07e749178c26","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"afca6772ad1d0c8df","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QbpjnowHkrvkhiMJdA8tuk","type":"tool_result","content":"      typeof insertError === \"object\" &&\n      \"code\" in insertError &&\n      (insertError as { code: string }).code === \"23505\"\n    ) {\n      console.log(\"License already inserted by concurrent request\");\n      return;\n    }\n    console.error(\"Failed to store license key:\", insertError);\n    throw insertError;\n  }\n\n  console.log(`License key stored in database for ${customerEmail}`);\n\n  // STEP 4b: Grant initial credits\n  if (insertedLicense) {\n    try {\n      await grantCreditLot({\n        userId: insertedLicense.userId,\n        amount: 5000,\n        sourceType: \"license_bundle\",\n        sourceId: session.id,\n      });\n      console.log(`Granted 5000 initial credits for license ${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}...`);\n    } catch (creditError) {\n      console.error(\"Failed to create initial credit balance:\", creditError);\n      // Don't throw - license was created, credits can be added later\n    }\n  }\n\n  // STEP 5: Send license email\n  await sendLicenseEmail(customerName, customerEmail, licenseKey);\n  // key should top that key up, not get a second key with a split balance.\n  // Only when the session hasn't already resolved a license (retry-safe), and\n  // only for granted keys (a revoked key is dead — mint a fresh one instead).\n  let pooledIntoExisting = false;\n  if (!license) {\n    // An email can own several keys (e.g. a revoked key plus a live one), so\n    // scan all of them newest-first and pool into the most recent GRANTED key.\n    // (findFirst-by-email could hand back the dead revoked row and wrongly mint\n    // a second key with a split balance.)\n    const existingKeys = await getAccountKeysByEmail(\n      customerEmail.toLowerCase().trim()\n    );\n    const existing = existingKeys.find((k) => k.status === \"granted\");\n    if (existing) {\n      license = existing;\n      pooledIntoExisting = true;\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!license) {\n    // Generate a unique license key with collision check.\n    return;\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Revoking license for checkout session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n\n  // STEP 5: Revoke the license in database\n  const license = await findAccountByStripeSession(checkoutSession.id);\n\n  if (!license) {\n    console.error(`License not found for session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  await refundCreditGrant({\n    sourceType: \"license_bundle\",\n    sourceId: checkoutSession.id,\n  });\n\n  // Already revoked - {"parentUuid":"0f286d35-6a07-459a-9bf6-bbf9a21b41b1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"afca6772ad1d0c8df","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VaHjpPg6B41fjZ5Ea7pARv","type":"tool_result","content":"473\texport async function handleChargeRefunded(\n474\t  charge: Stripe.Charge,\n475\t  eventId: string\n476\t): Promise<void> {\n477\t  console.log(\n478\t    `Processing refund for charge ${charge.id} (${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount})`\n479\t  );\n480\t\n481\t  // STEP 2: Get PaymentIntent ID from the charge\n482\t  const paymentIntentId =\n483\t    typeof charge.payment_intent === \"string\"\n484\t      ? charge.payment_intent\n485\t      : charge.payment_intent?.id;\n486\t\n487\t  if (!paymentIntentId) {\n488\t    console.log(\n489\t      \"No payment_intent on charge, skipping (likely not a Checkout purchase)\"\n490\t    );\n491\t    return;\n492\t  }\n493\t\n494\t  // STEP 3: Find the Checkout Session associated with this PaymentIntent\n495\t  const sessions = await stripe.checkout.sessions.list({\n496\t    payment_intent: paymentIntentId,\n497\t    limit: 1,\n498\t  });\n499\t\n500\t  if (sessions.data.length === 0) {\n501\t    console.log(\n502\t      `No checkout session found for payment_intent ${paymentIntentId}`\n503\t    );\n504\t    return;\n505\t  }\n506\t\n507\t  const checkoutSession = sessions.data[0];\n508\t  const purchaseType = checkoutSession.metadata?.purchase_type;\n509\t\n510\t  // STEP 4: Route by purchase type, applying the right \"is this refund\n511\t  // actionable?\" rule for each.\n512\t  if (purchaseType === \"credits\") {\n513\t    // The 6% fee is a separate, non-refundable line item, so a credit refund\n514\t    // refunds only the credit value — a partial Stripe refund. Act once the\n515\t    // refunded amount covers the credit portion (charge total minus the fee).\n516\t    const feeCents = parseInt(checkoutSession.metadata?.fee_cents || \"0\", 10);\n517\t    const creditPortion = charge.amount - feeCents;\n518\t    if (charge.amount_refunded < creditPortion) {\n519\t      console.log(\n520\t        `Refund ${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount} does not cover the credit portion (${creditPortion}) for credits session ${checkoutSession.id}, skipping`\n521\t      );\n522\t      return;\n523\t    }\n524\t    await handleCreditRefund(charge, checkoutSession, eventId);\n525\t    return;\n526\t  }\n527\t\n528\t  if (purchaseType !== \"license\") {\n529\t    console.log(\n530\t      `Refund for unknown purchase type (${purchaseType}), skipping`\n531\t    );\n532\t    return;\n533\t  }\n534\t\n535\t  // License purchases: act only on a FULL refund.\n536\t  if (charge.amount_refunded !== charge.amount) {\n537\t    console.log(\n538\t      `Partial refund (${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount}) for license session ${checkoutSession.id}, skipping`\n539\t    );\n540\t    return;\n541\t  }\n542\t\n543\t  console.log(`Revoking license for checkout session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n544\t\n545\t  // STEP 5: Revoke the license in database\n546\t  const license = await findAccountByStripeSession(checkoutSession.id);\n547\t\n548\t  if (!license) {\n549\t    console.error(`License not found for session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n550\t    return;\n551\t  }\n552\t\n553\t  await refundCreditGrant({\n554\t    sourceType: \"license_bundle\",\n555\t    sourceId: checkoutSession.id,\n556\t  });\n557\t\n558\t  // Already revoked - idempotent, just log and return\n559\t  if (license.status === \"revoked\") {\n560\t    console.log(`License ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... already revoked`);\n561\t    return;\n562\t  }\n563\t\n564\t  // STEP 6: Update status to revoked\n565\t  await updateAccountKey(license.id, { status: \"revoked\" });\n566\t\n567\t  console.log(\n568\t    `License ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... revoked due to full refund`\n569\t  );\n570\t}\n571\t\n572\t/**\n573\t * Reverse a refunded credit-pack purchase.\n574\t *\n575\t * Looks up the original credit grant from the checkout session metadata and\n576\t * atomically deducts it (clamped at 0). Idempotent via stripe_processed_events\n577\t * keyed by charge.id, so webhook retries never double-deduct.\n578\t */\n579\tasync function handleCreditRefund(\n580\t  charge: Stripe.Charge,\n581\t  checkoutSession: Stripe.Checkout.Session,\n582\t  eventId: string\n583\t): Promise<void> {\n584\t  // license_key is only present on top-ups; minted credit purchases carry none.\n585\t  // The clawback is keyed on the checkout session id (the grant's source_id),\n586\t  // so it works for both paths — license_key is informational only here.\n587\t  const licenseKey = checkoutSession.metadata?.license_key;"}]},"uuid":"7ad27d37-4012-474e-b711-90165e90a7cc","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:46:59.925Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/lib/services/stripe-webhook.ts","content":"export async function handleChargeRefunded(\n  charge: Stripe.Charge,\n  eventId: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  console.log(\n    `Processing refund for charge ${charge.id} (${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount})`\n  );\n\n  // STEP 2: Get PaymentIntent ID from the charge\n  const paymentIntentId =\n    typeof charge.payment_intent === \"string\"\n      ? charge.payment_intent\n      : charge.payment_intent?.id;\n\n  if (!paymentIntentId) {\n    console.log(\n      \"No payment_intent on charge, skipping (likely not a Checkout purchase)\"\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // STEP 3: Find the Checkout Session associated with this PaymentIntent\n  const sessions = await stripe.checkout.sessions.list({\n    payment_intent: paymentIntentId,\n    limit: 1,\n  });\n\n  if (sessions.data.length === 0) {\n    console.log(\n      `No checkout session found for payment_intent ${paymentIntentId}`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const checkoutSession = sessions.data[0];\n  const purchaseType = checkoutSession.metadata?.purchase_type;\n\n  // STEP 4: Route by purchase type, applying the right \"is this refund\n  // actionable?\" rule for each.\n  if (purchaseType === \"credits\") {\n    // The 6% fee is a separate, non-refundable line item, so a credit refund\n    // refunds only the credit value — a partial Stripe refund. Act once the\n    // refunded amount covers the credit portion (charge total minus the fee).\n    const feeCents = parseInt(checkoutSession.metadata?.fee_cents || \"0\", 10);\n    const creditPortion = charge.amount - feeCents;\n    if (charge.amount_refunded < creditPortion) {\n      console.log(\n        `Refund ${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount} does not cover the credit portion (${creditPortion}) for credits session ${checkoutSession.id}, skipping`\n      );\n      return;\n    }\n    await handleCreditRefund(charge, checkoutSession, eventId);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (purchaseType !== \"license\") {\n    console.log(\n      `Refund for unknown purchase type (${purchaseType}), skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // License purchases: act only on a FULL refund.\n  if (charge.amount_refunded !== charge.amount) {\n    console.log(\n      `Partial refund (${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount}) for license session ${checkoutSession.id}, skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Revoking license for checkout session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n\n  // STEP 5: Revoke the license in database\n  const license = await findAccountByStripeSession(checkoutSession.id);\n\n  if (!license) {\n    console.error(`License not found for session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  await refundCreditGrant({\n    sourceType: \"license_bundle\",\n    sourceId: checkoutSession.id,\n  });\n\n  // Already revoked - idempotent, just log and return\n  if (license.status === \"revoked\") {\n    console.log(`License ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... already revoked`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // STEP 6: Update status to revoked\n  await updateAccountKey(license.id, { status: \"revoked\" });\n\n  console.log(\n    `License ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... revoked due to full refund`\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Reverse a refunded credit-pack purchase.\n *\n * Looks up the original credit grant from the checkout session metadata and\n * atomically deducts it (clamped at 0). Idempotent via stripe_processed_events\n * keyed by charge.id, so webhook retries never double-deduct.\n */\nasync function handleCreditRefund(\n  charge: Stripe.Charge,\n  checkoutSession: Stripe.Checkout.Session,\n  eventId: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  // license_key is only present on top-ups; minted credit purchases carry none.\n  // The clawback is keyed on the checkout session id (the grant's source_id),\n  // so it works for both paths — license_key is informational only here.\n  const licenseKey = checkoutSession.metadata?.license_key;","numLines":115,"startLine":473,"totalLines":617}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0f286d35-6a07-459a-9bf6-bbf9a21b41b1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"d30b585d-823f-4966-b520-f9ae1478b9fb","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"afca6772ad1d0c8df","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWJHYAxFS8WmNPUWFhoz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"## T13 — admin tRPC surface: seam findings\n\n---\n\n### BUG 1 — \"Refund Only\" cannot keep the license: the Stripe webhook the refund itself fires revokes it anyway\n\n**trace**\n1. `nextjs/app/[locale]/user/(authenticated)/customers/CustomersClient.tsx:578-586` — \"Refund Only\" button → `refund.mutate({ licenseKeyId, revokeLicense: false })`\n2. `nextjs/server/api/routers/admin/customers.ts:323-326` — `stripe.refunds.create({ payment_intent })` with **no `amount`** → full refund\n3. `customers.ts:337-341` — `if (revokeLicense)` skipped; returns `{ success: true, revoked: false }`; UI at `CustomersClient.tsx:553-555` prints \"Refund processed successfully\" with no revoke wording\n4. Stripe delivers `charge.refunded` → `nextjs/app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:121-124` → `nextjs/lib/services/stripe-webhook.ts:473`\n5. `stripe-webhook.ts:536` — `charge.amount_refunded !== charge.amount` is **false** (step 2 refunded in full) → does not skip\n6. `stripe-webhook.ts:546-565` — `findAccountByStripeSession` → `updateAccountKey(license.id, { status: \"revoked\" })`\n\n**seam** EP-13 (`admin.customers.refund`) ↔ `handleChargeRefunded`. EP-13 assumes it is the only writer of `account_keys.status` for this refund; the webhook assumes every full refund it sees was *intended* as a revocation (true for customer-initiated dashboard refunds, false for an admin \"Refund Only\"). Neither side carries an intent marker (no metadata on the refund, no `stripe_processed_events` guard on the license branch).\n\n**trigger_sketch** Admin opens a license backed by a Stripe checkout with `metadata.purchase_type === \"license\"`, clicks **Refund Only**. Seconds later the webhook lands.\n\n**consequence** The customer's license is revoked despite the admin explicitly choosing not to revoke: `/sign-in/license-key` rejects it (`auth-license-key-plugin.ts:30` requires `status === \"granted\"`) and license validation fails, so the desktop app stops working. The admin UI told them the opposite. Requires admin privileges to trigger, but the damage lands on a customer and is invisible to the admin (the list refetch at 1.5 s may even race the webhook and show the stale \"granted\" row).\n\n**confidence** high\n\n---\n\n### BUG 2 — `admin.customers.grant` writes a non-normalized email into `account_keys`; the customer's own dashboard then finds zero licenses\n\n**trace**\n1. `CustomersClient.tsx:226-228` — `grantMutation.mutate({ email: grantEmail.trim() })` (trimmed, **not** lowercased)\n2. `customers.ts:196` — `z.object({ email: z.string().email() })`; zod v4 `.email()` does not normalize case\n3. `customers.ts:211` — `getOrCreateUser(email, …)` → `db-layer.ts:938,953` lowercases → `user.email = \"foo@bar.com\"`\n4. `customers.ts:217-222` — `insertAccountKey({ key, email, … })` with the **raw** input → `db-layer.ts:115` stores `account_keys.email = \"Foo@Bar.com\"`; `src/db/schema/account-keys.ts:21` is `text`, not `citext`\n5. Customer signs in → `server/api/routers/customer.ts:31,40` (`ctx.user.email?.toLowerCase()`) → `db-layer.ts:186-188` `eq(accountKeys.email, email.toLowerCase())` → **0 rows**\n\n**seam** EP-16 ↔ `getAccountKeysByEmail`. The reader tightened its contract (\"emails in this column are lowercase\" — it lowercases the *needle* but not the *haystack*); EP-16 is the one writer that never normalized. Note `db-layer.ts:245-270 provisionAccountKeyForEmail` — the sibling mint path — *does* normalize (`:246`), as does `updateCustomerEmail` (`db-layer.ts:1009`). EP-16 is the odd one out.\n\n**trigger_sketch** Admin types `Ray.Amjad@Gmail.com` into the Grant modal (any capital letter suffices) and grants.\n\n**consequence** Customer-visible, no admin required after the grant: `customer.licensesWithCredits`/`credits` (`customer.ts:40,98,144,179,200`) and `/api/customer/profile` (`route.ts:36`) all return an empty license list and **0 credits**, so the web dashboard says \"no license\" while the key works in the desktop app and while `admin.customers.list` (which displays `user.email` and searches with `ilike`, `db-layer.ts:222`) shows it as present. Also makes `stripe-webhook.ts:324` fail to pool a later purchase into the existing key, minting a second key. Silent — nothing errors.\n\n**confidence** high\n\n---\n\n### BUG 3 — Postgres `23505` detection is dead code under drizzle-orm 0.45.2 (and the fallback echoes the SQL + bound params)\n\n**trace**\n1. `customers.ts:169` — `await updateCustomerEmail(...)`\n2. `db-layer.ts:1010-1022` — genuine single transaction (`db.transaction(async (tx) => { tx.update(user)…; tx.update(accountKeys)… })`), so the partial-write worry is unfounded — both rows commit or neither does\n3. `node_modules/drizzle-orm/pg-core/session.js:36-98` — **every** query error is rethrown as `new DrizzleQueryError(queryString, params, e)`\n4. `node_modules/drizzle-orm/errors.js:10-19` — `DrizzleQueryError` has **no `.code`**; `message` = `` `Failed query: <full SQL>\\nparams: <all bound values>` ``; the pg error is only on `.cause`\n5. `customers.ts:172-178` — `(error as { code?: string })?.code === \"23505\"` → **never true** → falls through to `:180-184`, which returns `error.message` to the client\n\n**seam** `updateCustomerEmail` (throws a *wrapped* error) ↔ EP-15's error handler (expects the *raw* node-postgres error shape). The dependency upgrade moved the contract; the caller never learned.\n\n**trigger_sketch** Two admins (or one double-click) rename two different customers to `same@example.com` concurrently, so the loser slips past the pre-check at `customers.ts:160-166` and hits the `user.email` unique constraint.\n\n**consequence** Instead of `CONFLICT \"That email already belongs to another account\"`, the admin gets a 500 whose message is `Failed query: update \"user\" set \"email\" = $1 … params: same@example.com,false,…` rendered verbatim in the modal. Admin-only surface, so the param leak is **low** severity — I'd rate this on the broken error contract, not the leak. Same dead branch exists on a **non-admin** path: `stripe-webhook.ts:105-115` uses the identical `\"code\" in insertError` check as its concurrent-insert dedupe, so a genuine 23505 there now rethrows (`:118`) and Stripe retries the whole purchase handler instead of returning cleanly — worth a separate look outside T13.\n\nThis also answers the \"error-message echo\" question for EP-38 (`customers.ts:128`), EP-40 (`devices.ts:62`), EP-41 (`devices.ts:35`) and EP-42 (`stats.ts:76`): what lands there is the full SQL text plus every bound parameter. For `list`/`searchAccountKeysByEmail` the params are the admin's own search string; for the device queries, a timestamp. No other customer's data is in the params of those particular statements, and the surface is admin-gated — **honest verdict: low severity, not worth fixing on its own**, but it is the reason Bug 3's message is so noisy.\n\n**confidence** high (dead branch) / medium (race frequency)\n\n---\n\n### BUG 4 — `admin.customers.addCredits` accepts sub-cent amounts that Postgres silently rounds to nothing, then reports success\n\n**trace**\n1. `customers.ts:651,686` — free-text field, `parseFloat(creditAmount)`, only `> 0` checked client-side\n2. `customers.ts:253-256` — `z.number().positive().max(1_000_000)`; verified with the installed zod 4.4.3 that `0.001`, `1e-7`, `0.005` all pass (contrast `app/api/license/credits/validation.ts:18`, which rejects anything that does not round-trip at 2 dp — and documents exactly this hazard)\n3. `db-layer.ts:380-391` — `originalAmount/remainingAmount: amount.toString()` into `numeric(20,2)` (`src/db/schema/credit-grants.ts:22-25`) → `0.001` stored as `0.00`\n4. `db-layer.ts:354-366` — `balance + 0.001` into `numeric(20,2)` (`credit-balances.ts:11`) → `+0.00`\n5. `db-layer.ts:687` — the balance read filters `remaining_amount > 0`, so the new grant row never counts\n6. `customers.ts:279-284` — returns `addedAmount: 0.001` with `newBalance === previousBalance`\n\n**seam** EP-14's zod schema ↔ the `numeric(_, 2)` column. Two writers of the same column disagree: the REST path (`/api/license/credits`) enforces scale ≤ 2, the admin path does not. Both were individually reasonable.\n\n**trigger_sketch** Admin types `0.001` (or pastes `1e-7`) into Add Credits and submits. The modal's own preview at `CustomersClient.tsx:651` shows `= 0 minutes`, then the mutation reports success and the balance is unchanged.\n\n**consequence** Silent no-op dressed as success, plus a junk `credit_grants` row occupying the `(source_type, source_id)` slot forever. `0.005` rounds *up* to `0.01`, so the grant can also be larger than requested. Admin-only, no money moves, no drift between the cache and the grant total (both round identically — I checked) — so **low** severity, but it is a real spec divergence between two paths writing one column.\n\n**confidence** medium-high\n\n---\n\n### BUG 5 — unbounded `days` produces an Invalid Date that throws inside the driver, surfacing as a 500\n\n**trace**\n1. `devices.ts:21` / `devices.ts:46` — `z.number().positive().optional()`, no `.max()`, no `.int()`\n2. `devices.ts:24` — `input?.days ?? 30`\n3. `db-layer.ts:833-835` (and `:862-864`) — `since.setDate(since.getDate() - sinceDays)`\n4. Verified in node: `days = 1e21` → `Invalid Date`; `gte(deviceValidations.lastValidatedAt, Invalid Date)` → drizzle's timestamp `mapToDriverValue` calls `.toISOString()` → `RangeError: Invalid time value`\n5. `devices.ts:27-36` catches → `INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR` with the raw message\n\n**seam** EP-41/EP-40's zod schema (guarantees \"> 0\") ↔ `getDeviceCountsPerLicense` (assumes \"a sane number of days\"). `positive()` is not the same guarantee.\n\n**trigger_sketch** `POST/GET /api/trpc/admin.devices.list?input={\"json\":{\"days\":1e21}}`. (`Infinity` is *not* a trigger — zod 4 rejects non-finite numbers; I confirmed this, so the superjson-Infinity angle is closed.)\n\nSecond, quieter variant on the same line: fractional days truncate. `days: 0.5` and `days: 0.0000001` both produce `getDate() - d` → `ToIntegerOrInfinity` truncation → a **full day** window. Any sub-day window silently becomes 1 day.\n\n**consequence** Admin-only 500 instead of a 400, with a confusing driver-level message. No data exposure, no full-table scan (the query never reaches Postgres). Low severity; the UI only ever sends `30` (`DevicesClient.tsx:20`).\n\n**confidence** high (mechanism), low (anyone actually doing it)\n\n---\n\n### BUG 6 — `admin.stats.get` renders provider outages as legitimate zeros\n\n**trace** `stats.ts:34-39` (`catch {}` around `stripe.customers.list`) and `stats.ts:44-58` (`catch {}` around the Polar pagination) → `stats.ts:65-70` returns `{ totalCustomers: 0, stripeCustomers: 0, polarCustomers: 0 }`.\n\n**seam** The provider clients (which signal failure only by throwing) ↔ EP-42's aggregation (which encodes \"no data\" and \"call failed\" as the same `0`). Nothing in the return shape carries a per-provider status, so the client at the dashboard has no way to distinguish them.\n\n**trigger_sketch** Unset/rotated `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`, a Stripe 5xx, or a network blip during the request. The comment `// Stripe not configured` shows the author only imagined the config case.\n\n**consequence** The admin dashboard confidently shows \"0 customers\" during an outage. Admin-only, read-only, no state corruption — but it is a genuine silent-wrong-output bug, and the only signal (`console.error` at `:72`) never fires because the inner catches swallow first. Separately, `stripe.customers.list({ limit: 100 })` is not paginated at all despite the docstring saying \"paginated\", so `stripeCustomers` saturates at 100 — the number is wrong even on the happy path.\n\n**confidence** high\n\n---\n\n### BUG 7 (low) — revoking a license does not end the customer's already-minted session\n\n**trace** `customers.ts:338` `updateAccountKey(id, { status: \"revoked\" })` → only `account_keys.status`. `auth-license-key-plugin.ts:63-74` minted a full Better Auth session via `internalAdapter.createSession` + `setSessionCookie`. Grepping the whole app for `revokeSessions`/`deleteSessions`/`internalAdapter` returns exactly one hit — the creation site. Nothing invalidates sessions.\n\n**seam** EP-13's revoke ↔ the license-key sign-in plugin. The plugin assumes license status is checked at *every* entry; the revoke assumes flipping status is sufficient.\n\n**trigger_sketch** Customer signs in with their key, keeps the tab open; admin refunds & revokes; customer keeps browsing `/user/dashboard`.\n\n**consequence** A refunded customer retains web-portal access until the session cookie expires. The desktop app is cut off (validation is by key), and the dashboard only shows their own data, so this is **low** severity — but it means \"revoked\" is not immediate.\n\n---\n\n### Checked and cleared (explicitly not bugs)\n\n- **The `adminProcedure` gate is sound.** `createTRPCContext:48-50` reads `role` off the server-verified Better Auth session; `src/lib/auth.ts:12-21` declares `role` with **`input: false`**, so it is not settable at sign-up — no privilege escalation. If `getSession` throws, `createTRPCContext` rejects and tRPC returns a 500 *before* any procedure runs (fail-closed). `role: undefined` → `isAdmin === false` → FORBIDDEN. `adminProcedure` uses only `isAdmin`, but `isAdmin` re-checks `ctx.user` at `:102-107` and throws before `next()`, so not composing `isAuthed` is harmless.\n- **No mutation is reachable over GET.** `@trpc/server` 10.45.3 `callProcedure` (`dist/config-61497ca3.js:133-143`) requires `procedures[path]._def[type]`; a GET fixes `type = 'query'`, so a mutation path returns `NOT_FOUND` before the middleware chain. The CSRF-mutation concern is closed.\n- **The admin queries are triggerable cross-site but not readable.** No CORS headers anywhere in the repo (grepped), no `trustedOrigins` config, and `middleware.ts`'s matcher explicitly excludes `/api`. So a logged-in admin can be *navigated* to `…/api/trpc/admin.customers.list?input=…` (Lax cookies ride top-level GETs) and the JSON renders in their own tab — but with no `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` the attacker's page cannot read it, and a cross-site `fetch` with `credentials:'include'` won't carry the Lax session cookie at all. Not a bug; noted because the PII/plaintext-key payload makes it worth never adding permissive CORS here.\n- **EP-16's key-collision loop (`customers.ts:203-208`) is correct.** Read literally: `key = generate(); existing = await find(key); if (!existing) break; if (i === 4) throw;`. The `break` precedes the `i === 4` check, so the only way to exit with a colliding key would be to fall out of the loop — impossible, because iteration 4 with a collision throws. Every emitted key was checked. And even the TOCTOU race between `findAccountByKey` and `insertAccountKey` is safe: `src/db/schema/account-keys.ts:42` declares `uniqueIndex(\"idx_account_keys_key\")`, so a concurrent duplicate raises 23505 at insert and the admin sees an error. **Cosmetic at worst, not severe** — the inventory's flag is a false positive.\n- **EP-15 is genuinely one transaction** (`db-layer.ts:1010-1022`, quoted above); `user.email` and the `account_keys.email` rows cannot diverge. It also correctly resets `emailVerified` (`:1016`). The moved email does re-point entitlement resolution — every `getAccountKeysByEmail` reader now resolves the *new* address, which is the intent.\n- **EP-13's non-atomicity is largely self-healing.** If the process dies after `refunds.create` but before `refundCreditGrant`/`updateAccountKey`, the `charge.refunded` webhook performs both (`stripe-webhook.ts:553-565`). On an admin retry, the Stripe idempotency key reuses the refund and `refundCreditGrant` returns `duplicate` (`db-layer.ts:479-481`) so no double-deduct; the DB writes rerun but are idempotent. One residual: Stripe idempotency keys expire after 24 h, so a retry the next day creates a *new* refund request and surfaces a raw `charge_already_refunded` Stripe error to the admin instead of a friendly message (low).\n- **EP-38's `ilike('%' + search + '%')`** (`db-layer.ts:222`) does not escape `%`/`_`, so a search for `%` matches everything — but that path is capped at 1000 and admin-gated, and the follow-on `getAccountKeysWithCreditsForUserIds` (`db-layer.ts:782-792`) is uncapped, so a 1000-row match fans out into an unbounded second query. Performance nuisance only.\n\n---\n\n### depth_limits\n\n- Did not execute anything against Postgres, Stripe or Polar — numeric-scale rounding is inferred from `numeric(20,2)` in the schema plus the `remaining_amount > 0` filter, and the `Invalid Date` path from a local node repro of the date arithmetic plus reading drizzle's `mapToDriverValue` contract rather than running the query.\n- Bug 1 relies on `checkoutSession.metadata.purchase_type === \"license\"` being set on the sessions the admin can refund; I read the refund-side branch but did not trace every checkout-creation site to confirm the metadata is universally present. If some license sessions lack it, the webhook skips at `stripe-webhook.ts:528-533` and those licenses survive — which would make the bug intermittent rather than universal, and arguably worse.\n- Did not audit `hyperwhisper-cloud/` (nothing in T13 pointed into it) nor the `download`/`customer` routers beyond the `getAccountKeysByEmail` call sites needed for Bug 2.\n- Did not verify Better Auth's actual cookie `sameSite` default from `node_modules` — the CSRF conclusion rests on the documented Lax default plus the absence of CORS headers; the \"not readable\" half of that conclusion holds regardless.\n- Bug 3's blast radius outside `admin/` (other `error.code === \"23505\"` / pg-error-shape checks across the app) was only spot-checked via the one `stripe-webhook.ts` hit.\n\n###
